# Every findings draft audited (chain 506, 19 November 2026)

Written by chain 506 on samueloldlaptop (a code machine: no data lock, no Stata, no model calls). One row a draft of [`docs/findings/`](https://github.com/SamuelEnrique/erw/blob/main/docs/findings), all 549 of them; the 364 written on 19 November 2026 by the desktop's second window (chain 502) are marked "19 Nov". The chain's rules: [`queue/done/506-day-19-november-oldlaptop-findings-audit-shortlist.md`](https://github.com/SamuelEnrique/erw/blob/main/queue/done/506-day-19-november-oldlaptop-findings-audit-shortlist.md). The sheet of forty drawn from it: [`docs/reviews/findings-shortlist-2026-11-19.md`](https://github.com/SamuelEnrique/erw/blob/main/docs/reviews/findings-shortlist-2026-11-19.md). The data behind every row (one CSV a check): [`docs/reviews/findings-audit-2026-11-19/`](https://github.com/SamuelEnrique/erw/blob/main/docs/reviews/findings-audit-2026-11-19).

## The four checks

1. **The sentence says only what the figure shows**: figure, unit, place, period, what it is compared with; no cause, no forecast, no advice; a superlative ("the first full year the ERW holds") true of the whole table, not of the rows the script read.
2. **The figure reproduces here**: the draft's `reproduce.py` run on this machine, its printed figures set against the draft's. The site's download (`https://erw-flame.vercel.app/api/download?table=...`) answered HTTP 403 on 19 November 2026 ("open to the internal view only"), so no public download exists to run from; each run read the ERW's own copy of the table (from `warehouse/output` on this machine, or rebuilt from the ERW's archive bucket by `warehouse/archive/restore.py --from-bucket` into `runs/`). A draft whose table is internal or held, or too large for this machine's memory, is "not checked", never "pass".
3. **The Stata file is true to the Python**: the same steps in the same order, and Samuel's rules (no line continuation, one empty line between commands outside loops and none inside, every variable destringed with `force`). Read, not run: this machine has no Stata.
4. **The chart says what it is**: title, axes and units, source line, period, read from the drawing code ([`warehouse/analysis/findings/deep/builder.py`](https://github.com/SamuelEnrique/erw/blob/main/warehouse/analysis/findings/deep/builder.py), one template for every draft) and the data file. Looks not seen.
